</sup>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>*yi*</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>戴上</rb><rt>ta-zông</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>体面个</rb><rt>tʽi-min-go</rt><rb>平天冠</rb><rt>bing-tʽin-kwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>掌管</rb><rt>công-kwun</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>造个</rb><rt>zao-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>万物</rb><rt>væn-veh</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>归服</rb><rt>kwe-voh</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>脚下</rb><rt>kyiah-ʽô</rt></ruby>。」 <ruby><rb>既然</rb><rt>Kyi-jün</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>万物</rb><rt>væn-veh</rt><rb>归服</rb><rt>kwe-voh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>」，<ruby><rb>断</rb><rt>tön</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>归服</rb><rt>kwe-voh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>如今</rb><rt>jü-kying</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>万物</rb><rt>væn-veh</rt><rb>归服</rb><rt>kwe-voh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>Tsih-yiu</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>暂时</rb><rt>dzæn-z</rt><rb>小点个</rb><rt>siao-tin-go</rt></ruby>」<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>死个</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>苦头</rb><rt>kwʽu-deo</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>戴上</rb><rt>ta-zông</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>体面个</rb><rt>tʽi-min-go</rt><rb>平天冠</rb><rt>bing-tʽin-kwun</rt></ruby>」，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>替</rb><rt>tʽi</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>死个</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>苦头</rb><rt>*kwʽu-deo*</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, shü-vi siao-tin. 或话，势微小点。（原注）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, Tsih kʽen-kyin Yiæ-su ta-zông yüong-wô tʽi-min-go bing-tʽin-kwun: Gyi we-leh si-go kwʽu-deo dzæn-z pi tʽin-s siao-tin-go. 或话，只看见<u>耶稣</u>带上荣华体面个平天冠。其为了死个苦头暂时比天使小点个。（原注）</sup>

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>万物</rb><rt>Væn-veh</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>带领</rb><rt>ta-ling</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>得救个</rb><rt>teh-kyiu-go</rt><rb>君王</rb><rt>Kyüing-wông</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>苦难</rb><rt>kwʽu-næn</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>全备</rb><rt>djün-be</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>应该个</rb><rt>ing-kæ-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>箇位</rb><rt>Keh-we</rt><rb>尊起</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*gyi-lah*</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>尊起个</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>出于</rb><rt>cʽih-ü</rt><rb>一位</rb><rt>Ih-we</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>称呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>惶恐</rb><rt>wông-kʽong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>12</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>通报</rb><rt>tʽong-pao</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>教会</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>唱</rb><rt>tsʽông</rt><rb>诗</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>赞美</rb><rt>tsæn-me</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。」 

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>血气</rb><rt>hyüih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其自</rb><rt>Gyi-zi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>成</rb><rt>dzing</rt><rb>为</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>血气</rb><rt>hyüih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>十字架个</rb><rt>*jih-z-kô-go*</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>败掉</rb><rt>ba-diao</rt><rb>掌管</rb><rt>công-kwun</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>魔鬼</rb><rt>mo-kwe</rt></ruby>；<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>释放</rb><rt>sih-fông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一生</rb><rt>ih-seng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>并</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>*læ*</rt><rb>扶助</rb><rt>vu-dzu</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>*læ*</rt><rb>扶助</rb><rt>vu-dzu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亚伯拉罕</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen-go</rt><rb>后代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>讲到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>慈悲</rb><rt>dz-pe</rt><rb>忠信个</rb><rt>cong-sing-go</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>Tsi-s-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>挽回</rb><rt>w̆æn-we</rt><rb>百姓个</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>相同</rb><rt>siang-dong</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>其自</rb><rt>Gyi-zi</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>受过</rb><rt>ziu-ko</rt><rb>试炼个</rb><rt>s-lin-go</rt><rb>苦难</rb><rt>kwʽu-næn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>帮助</rb><rt>pông-dzu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>试炼个</rb><rt>s-lin-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。

---

### H. III.

<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>KA-NI</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>召个</rb><rt>dziao-go</rt><rb>圣善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>仔细</rb><rt>ts-si</rt><rb>思忖</rb><rt>s-tsʽeng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>招认</rb><rt>tsiao-jing</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>*dao-li*</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>差使</rb><rt>Tsʽa-s</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>Tsi-s-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>忠信</rb><rt>cong-sing</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>*voh-z*</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>设立</rb><rt>shih-lih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>*Cü*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>「<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>合家</rb><rt>ʽeh-kô</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>*yiu*</rt><rb>忠信</rb><rt>*cong-sing*</rt></ruby>」<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。 <sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>况且</rb><rt>Hwông-tsʽia</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>过如</rb><rt>ko-jü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>犹之乎</rb><rt>yiu-ts-wu</rt><rb>成家个</rb><rt>dzing-kô-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt><rb>一家个</rb><rt>ih-kô-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>更加</rb><rt>keng-kô</rt><rb>贵重</rb><rt>kwe-djong</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>成家个</rb><rt>dzing-kô-go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>cü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>至于</rb><rt>ts-ü</rt><rb>成就</rb><rt>dzing-dziu</rt><rb>万物</rb><rt>væn-veh</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>」，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>将来</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>合家</rb><rt>ʽeh-kô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>忠信</rb><rt>cong-sing</rt></ruby>。」<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>治</rb><rt>djü</rt><rb>其自个</rb><rt>Gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>一家</rb><rt>ih-kô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>*yiu*</rt><rb>忠信</rb><rt>*cong-sing*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>放胆</rb><rt>fông-tæn</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>*siang-sing*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>高兴</rb><rt>kao-hying</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>底</rb><rt>ti</rt><rb>坚固</rb><rt>kyin-kwu</rt><rb>守住</rb><rt>siu-djü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>一家个</rb><rt>ih-kô-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>今末</rb><rt>Kyih-mih</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>硬</rb><rt>ngang</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>*zin-deo*</rt><rb>激</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>*Ngô*</rt><rb>𢛟惶</rb><rt>ô-wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>试试</rb><rt>s-s</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>*Ngô*</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>试试</rb><rt>s-s</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>探探</rb><rt>tʽen-tʽen</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>四十</rb><rt>s-jih</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>可恶</rb><rt>kʽo-u</rt><rb>箇代个</rb><rt>keh-dæ-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>一向</rb><rt>ih-hyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>邪僻个</rb><rt>zia-pʽih-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>识得</rb><rt>sih-teh</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>路头</rb><rt>lu-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>动怒</rb><rt>dong-nu</rt><rb>罚咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>安息</rb><rt>en-sih</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>Hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>顾着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>存</rb><rt>dzeng</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>凶恶个</rb><rt>hyüong-ôh-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>活个</rb><rt>weh-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> 「<ruby><rb>今末</rb><rt>kyih-mih</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>日打日</rb><rt>nyih-tang-nyih</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>相劝</rb><rt>siang-kyʽün</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>罪孽个</rb><rt>ze-nyih-go</rt><rb>哄骗</rb><rt>hong-pʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>变</rb><rt>pin</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>硬</rb><rt>ngang</rt><rb>心肠</rb><rt>*sing-dziang*</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>放胆</rb><rt>fông-tæn</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>*siang-sing*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>始终如一</rb><rt>s-cong-jü-ih</rt><rb>坚固</rb><rt>kyin-kwu</rt><rb>守住</rb><rt>siu-djü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>有份</rb><rt>yiu-veng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>上文</rb><rt>*Zông-veng*</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>今末</rb><rt>Kyih-mih</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>硬</rb><rt>ngang</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>*zin-deo*</rt><rb>激</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>𢛟惶</rb><rt>ô-wông</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>之后</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt><rb>激</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt><rb>𢛟惶</rb><rt>ô-wông</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>岂</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt><rb>四十</rb><rt>s-jih</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt><rb>可恶</rb><rt>kʽo-u</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>岂</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>犯了</rb><rt>væn-leh</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>尸首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt><rb>摊</rb><rt>tʽæn</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>旷野里</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ-li</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt><rb>罚咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>许</rb><rt>hyü</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>安息</rb><rt>en-sih</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>Ziu-z</rt><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>依顺个</rb><rt>i-jing-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>可</rb><rt>Kʽo</rt><rb>见得</rb><rt>kyin-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>缘故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt></ruby>。

### H. IV.

<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>安息</rb><rt>en-sih</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>应许</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>遗落东</rb><rt>yi-lôh-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>怕惧</rb><rt>pʽô-gyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>未免</rb><rt>vi-min</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>错落个</rb><rt>tsʽo-lôh-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>信息</rb><rt>sing-sih</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>传个</rb><rt>djün-go</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>安息</rb><rt>*en-sih*</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>无益</rb><rt>vu-ih</rt><rb>于</rb><rt>ü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>听闻个</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>存的</rb><rt>dzeng-tih</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>安息</rb><rt>en-sih</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>只有</rb><rt>*tsih-yiu*</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>走进去</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>动怒</rb><rt>dong-nu</rt><rb>罚咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>安息</rb><rt>en-sih</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>。」 <ruby><rb>然而</rb><rt>jün-r</rt><rb>天地</rb><rt>tʽin-di</rt><rb>造成</rb><rt>zao-dzing</rt><rb>之后</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>*Jing-ming-go*</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>完工了</rb><rt>wun-kong-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>讲到</rb><rt>Kông-tao</rt><rb>第七</rb><rt>di-tsʽih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>*nyih*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一片</rb><rt>ih-pʽin</rt><rb>书</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>第七</rb><rt>di-tsʽih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>完工</rb><rt>wun-kong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>安息了</rb><rt>en-sih-de</rt></ruby>。」 <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>造个</rb><rt>zao-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>隐瞒</rb><rt>ing-mun</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>万物</rb><rt>væn-veh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>眼前</rb><rt>ngæn-zin</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>出露露</rb><rt>cʽih-lu-lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>明明</rb><rt>ming-ming</rt><rb>摆东</rb><rt>pa-tong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>结算</rb><rt>kyih-sön</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>账</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>*Cü*</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Feh i-jing-go; *yiu-teh* *fæn*, feh siang-sing-go. 弗依顺个；有得翻，弗相信个。（原注）</sup>

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>KA</rt><rb>看起来</rb><rt>kʽen-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>Tsi-s-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>经过</rb><rt>kying-ko</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>坚固</rb><rt>kyin-kwu</rt><rb>守住</rb><rt>siu-djü</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>*ah-lah*</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>招认个</rb><rt>tsiao-jing-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>况且</rb><rt>Hwông-tsʽia</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>Tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>再𣍐</rb><rt>tsæ-ve</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>体谅</rb><rt>tʽi-liang</rt><rb>哀怜</rb><rt>æ-lin</rt><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>懦弱</rb><rt>no-ziah</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>试炼</rb><rt>s-lin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>*tsih-z*</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>遭着</rb><rt>tsao-djôh</rt><rb>罪愆</rb><rt>ze-kyʽin</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>放胆</rb><rt>fông-tæn</rt><rb>走拢</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt><rb>鸿恩</rb><rt>*ʽong*-eng</rt><rb>座位</rb><rt>zo-we</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>哀怜</rb><rt>æ-lin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>占着</rb><rt>tsin-djôh</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>随时</rb><rt>ze-z</rt><rb>帮助</rb><rt>pông-dzu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>*ah-lah*</rt></ruby>。

### H. V.

<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>拣选出来</rb><rt>kæn-shün-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>替</rb><rt>tʽi</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>*voh-z*</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>设立个</rb><rt>shih-lih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>替</rb><rt>tʽi</rt><rb>罪恶</rb><rt>ze-ôh</rt><rb>献上</rb><rt>hyin-zông</rt><rb>礼物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>祭祀</rb><rt>tsi-z</rt></ruby>；<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>原谅</rb><rt>nyün-liang</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>岔路</rb><rt>tsʽô-lu</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>和然</rb><rt>ʽo-jün</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>懦弱个</rb><rt>no-ziah-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>当该</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>赎罪个</rb><rt>joh-ze-go</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>贵重个</rb><rt>kwe-djong-go</rt><rb>职份</rb><rt>tsih-veng</rt><rb>呒人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>敢</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>当</rb><rt>tông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>蒙</rb><rt>mong</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>召来</rb><rt>dziao-læ</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>推尊</rb><rt>tʽe-tseng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>*sô*</rt><rb>推尊个</rb><rt>*tʽe-tseng-go*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>今末</rb><rt>kyih-mih</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>别</rb><rt>bih</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>*u-sen*</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>永远</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>*tso*</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>Tsi-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>麦基洗德</u>个</rb><rt>Mah-kyi-si-teh-go</rt><rb>班次</rb><rt>pæn-tsʽ</rt></ruby>。」 

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>成</rb><rt>dzing</rt><rb>为</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>忏念</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyiæn</rt><rb>求恳</rb><rt>gyiu-kʽeng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>眼泪</rb><rt>ngæn-li</rt><rb>浡出</rb><rt>beh-cʽih</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>声响</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>免掉</rb><rt>min-diao</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>尊敬</rb><rt>tseng-kying</rt><rb>怕惧个</rb><rt>pʽô-gyü-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>虽即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>依顺</rb><rt>i-jing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>苦难</rb><rt>kwʽu-næn</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>习练过来个</rb><rt>jih-lin-ko-læ-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>Tao-leh</rt><rb>全备</rb><rt>djün-be</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>*ziu*</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>依顺</rb><rt>i-jing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>永远</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>得救个</rb><rt>teh-kyiu-go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>称呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>Tsi-s-deo</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>麦基洗德</u>个</rb><rt>Mah-kyi-si-teh-go</rt><rb>班次</rb><rt>pæn-tsʽ</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, *Jing-ming* ziu tʽing Gyi, *s-teh* *Gyi* tʽeh-cʽih pʽô-gyü-go sing: *ʽôh*, *Jing-ming* ziu tʽing Gyi, *kyiu* *Gyi* min-diao Gyi sô pʽô-gyü-go z-ken. *Kʽen* Mt. 26. 37. Mk. 14. 33. Iʽe. 12. 27. 或话，或话，神明就听其，使得其脱出怕惧个心。或，神明就听其，救其免掉其所怕惧个事干。看 Mt. 26. 37. Mk. 14. 33. Iʽe. 12. 27.（原注）</sup>

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>讲到</rb><rt>KÔNG-TAO</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>难</rb><rt>næn</rt><rb>解说</rb><rt>ka-shih</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>听仔</rb><rt>tʽing-ts</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>茫无头绪了</rb><rt>mông-vu-deo-jü-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>论</rb><rt>leng</rt><rb>时候个</rb><rt>z-ʽeo-go</rt><rb>长久</rb><rt>*dziang-kyiu*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>原</rb><rt>*nyün*</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>却勒</rb><rt>kyʽiah-leh</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>诏书个</rb><rt>tsiao-shü-go</rt><rb>初学</rb><rt>tsʽu-ʽôh</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>却勒</rb><rt>kyʽiah-leh</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>*kyʽüoh*</rt><rb>奶</rb><rt>na</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>饭食</rb><rt>væn-zih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>吃弗来了</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-feh-læ-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>吃奶个</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-na-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>通达</rb><rt>tʽong-dah</rt><rb>公义个</rb><rt>kong-yi-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>Tsih-yiu</rt><rb>成人长大个</rb><rt>dzing-nying-tsiang-da-go</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>*kyʽüoh*</rt><rb>饭食</rb><rt>væn-zih</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>见识</rb><rt>kyin-sih</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>习练</rb><rt>jih-lin</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>能够辨别</rb><rt>neng-keo-bin-pih</rt><rb>好歹</rb><rt>hao-tæ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, djün-be-go cü-kwu; *kʽen* 1K. 2. 6. H. 3. 15: *ʽôh*, wun-djün-go; *kʽen* Yk. 4. 13. 或话，全备个主顾；看 1K. 2. 6. H. 3. 15。或，完全个；看 Yk. 4. 13.（原注）</sup>

### HI. VI.

<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基督</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>初学</rb><rt>tsʽu-ʽôh</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>渐渐</rb><rt>*dzin-dzin*</rt><rb>进益</rb><rt>tsing-ih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>全备个</rb><rt>djün-be-go</rt><rb>地步</rb><rt>di-bu</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>底子</rb><rt>ti-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>*ziu-z*</rt><rb>悔改</rb><rt>hwe-kæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt><rb>死个</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>行为</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>领受</rb><rt>*ling-ziu*</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>按手个</rb><rt>en-siu-go</rt><rb>礼节</rb><rt>*li-tsih*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>永远</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>判断</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>应许</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, ah-lah hao-vong kông Kyi-toh-go tsʽu-ʽôh dao-li; hao kyʽi kông keh djün-be-go dao-li. 或话，阿拉好甮讲<u>基督</u>个初学道理；好去讲箇全备个道理。（原注）</sup>

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>倘然</rb><rt>Tʽông-jün</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>一遭</rb><rt>ih-tsao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>光明</rb><rt>kwông-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尝着</rb><rt>zông-djôh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>恩赐个</rb><rt>eng-s-go</rt><rb>味道</rb><rt>mi-dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>圣灵个</rb><rt>Sing-Ling-go</rt><rb>感动</rb><rt>*ken-dong*</rt></ruby>，<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>尝着</rb><rt>zông-djôh</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>好好</rb><rt>hao-hao</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>将来</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>时世个</rb><rt>z-shü-go</rt><rb>能干</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt></ruby>，<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>*ʽeo-deo*</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>失掉</rb><rt>shih-diao</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>*dao-li*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>断</rb><rt>tön</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>回心转意</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i</rt><rb>一礼</rb><rt>ih-li</rt><rb>做人</rb><rt>tso-nying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>*keh*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>*z*</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>重先</rb><rt>djong-sin</rt><rb>钉煞</rb><rt>ting-sah</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>明明</rb><rt>ming-ming</rt><rb>糟蹋</rb><rt>tsao-tʽah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>譬如</rb><rt>Pʽi-jü</rt><rb>田地</rb><rt>din-di</rt><rb>不常</rb><rt>peh-djông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>雨</rb><rt>yü</rt><rb>滋润</rb><rt>ts-jing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>发出</rb><rt>fah-cʽih</rt><rb>土产</rb><rt>tʽu-tsʽæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>合着</rb><rt>ʽeh-djôh</rt><rb>农夫个</rb><rt>nong-fu-go</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>用场</rb><rt>yüong-dziang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>朵化</rb><rt>tô-hwô</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>生出</rb><rt>sang-cʽih</rt><rb>刺棚</rb><rt>tsʽ-bang</rt><rb>野草</rb><rt>yia-tsʽao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>*ziu*</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>*iao*</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>弃掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>近乎</rb><rt>gying-wu</rt><rb>咒骂</rb><rt>tsiu-mô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>归根</rb><rt>kwe-keng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>𤈦掉</rb><rt>me-diao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, kang-cong cü-nying-kô-go. 或话，耕种主人家个。（原注）</sup>

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>亲爱个</rb><rt>Tsʽing-æ-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>*hyüong-di*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>虽即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>实惠</rb><rt>jih-we</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>各路个</rb><rt>kôh-lu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>再𣍐</rb><rt>tsæ-ve</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>公道</rb><rt>kong-dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>忘记</rb><rt>mông-kyi</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>仁爱个</rb><rt>jing-æ-go</rt><rb>劳碌</rb><rt>lao-loh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>显出个</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih-go</rt><rb>仁爱</rb><rt>*jing-æ*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>素常</rb><rt>su-djông</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>供应</rb><rt>kong-ing</rt><rb>圣徒</rb><rt>sing-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>现在</rb><rt>*yin-dzæ*</rt><rb>仍旧</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>巴弗能够</rb><rt>pô-feh-neng-keo</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>显出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>做样</rb><rt>tso-yiang</rt><rb>热心</rb><rt>nyih-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>抵</rb><rt>ti</rt><rb>笃实</rb><rt>toh-jih</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>懈怠</rb><rt>yiæ-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>*iao*</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>忍耐</rb><rt>jing-næ</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>接着</rb><rt>tsih-djôh</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>应许</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt><rb>主顾个</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>样</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>应许</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>更加</rb><rt>keng-kô</rt><rb>大个</rb><rt>do-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>指点</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>罚咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>指点</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>罚咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>14</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>昌盛</rb><rt>cʽông-zing</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>*ts-seng*</rt></ruby>。」<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伯拉罕</rb><rt>*Üô-pah-lah-hen*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>耐心</rb><rt>næ-sing</rt><rb>等待</rb><rt>teng-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>应许个</rb><rt>ing-hyü-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>*Væn-pah*</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>罚咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>指点</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>大如</rb><rt>do-jü</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>*gyi-zi*</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>罚咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>靠实</rb><rt>kʽao-jih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>*shih-wô*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>呒告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>转变</rb><rt>cün-pin</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>巴弗能够</rb><rt>pô-feh-neng-keo</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>接着</rb><rt>tsih-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt><rb>应许</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>明明</rb><rt>ming-ming</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>旨意</rb><rt>ts-i</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>更改弗来</rb><rt>keng-kæ-feh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>*sô-yi*</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>罚咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>凭据</rb><rt>bing-kyü</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>两样</rb><rt>liang-yiang</rt><rb>更改弗来个</rb><rt>keng-kæ-feh-læ-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>食言</rb><rt>zih-yin</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>逃难</rb><rt>dao-næn</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>指点</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>安安心心</rb><rt>en-en-sing-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>*siang-vông*</rt><rb>可比</rb><rt>kʽo-pi</rt><rb>灵魂个</rb><rt>ling-weng-go</rt><rb>锚</rb><rt>mao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>着实</rb><rt>dziah-jih</rt><rb>牢靠</rb><rt>lao-kʽao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>抛</rb><rt>pʽao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>幔帐</rb><rt>mun-tsiang</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>先锋</rb><rt>sin-fong</rt><rb>替</rb><rt>tʽi</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>*sin*</rt><rb>走进去</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>麦基洗德</u>个</rb><rt>Mah-kyi-si-teh-go</rt><rb>班次</rb><rt>pæn-tsʽ</rt></ruby>」<ruby><rb>永远</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>Tsi-s-deo</rt></ruby>。

### H. VII.

<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>KEH</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>麦基洗德</rb><rt>Mah-kyi-si-teh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb><u>撒冷</u>个</rb><rt>Sah-leng-go</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>高</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>杀败了</rb><rt>sah-ba-leh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>国王</rb><rt>koh-wông</rt><rb>转来</rb><rt>cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>；<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亚伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>一切个</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>十股得一</rb><rt>jih-kwu-teh-ih</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb><u>麦基洗德</u>个</rb><rt>*Mah-kyi-si-teh-go*</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>*ming-z*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>第一</rb><rt>di-ih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>公义</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>」<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>解说</rb><rt>ka-shih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>*kyiao-leh*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒冷</rb><rt>Sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>平安个</rb><rt>bing-en-go</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>」；<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>家谱</rb><rt>kô-pu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>生日</rb><rt>sang-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>死期</rb><rt>si-gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>；——<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>长通</rb><rt>dziang-tʽong</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>忖忖看</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tsʽeng-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>祖宗头</rb><rt>tsu-tsong-deo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>掳掠来个</rb><rt>lo-liah-læ-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>十股得一</rb><rt>jih-kwu-teh-ih</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋样</rb><rt>dza-nyiang</rt><rb>尊贵</rb><rt>tseng-kwe</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>职份</rb><rt>tsih-veng</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>虽即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>出于</rb><rt>cʽih-ü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亚伯拉罕</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen-go</rt><rb>骨肉</rb><rt>kweh-joh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>然而</rb><rt>jün-r</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>奉旨</rb><rt>vong-ts</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>百姓个</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>十股得一</rb><rt>jih-kwu-teh-ih</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>麦基洗德</rb><rt>Mah-kyi-si-teh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亚伯拉罕</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>十股得一</rb><rt>jih-kwu-teh-ih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>应许</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>下辈</rb><rt>ʽô-pe</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>上辈个</rb><rt>zông-pe-go</rt><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>呒扳驳个</rb><rt>m-pæn-poh-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>*dziu*</rt><rb>荡边</rb><rt>dông-pin</rt><rb>看来</rb><rt>*kʽen-læ*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>驮归</rb><rt>do-kyü</rt><rb>十股得一</rb><rt>jih-kwu-teh-ih</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>*dziu*</rt><rb>箇边</rb><rt>keh-pin</rt><rb>看来</rb><rt>*kʽen-læ*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>*keh*</rt><rb>驮归个</rb><rt>*do-kyü-go*</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>*cü-kwu*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>活个</rb><rt>weh-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>虽即</rb><rt>*se-tsih*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>驮归</rb><rt>do-kyü</rt><rb>十股得一</rb><rt>jih-kwu-teh-ih</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亚伯拉罕</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen-go</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>十股得一</rb><rt>jih-kwu-teh-ih</rt><rb>驮出了</rb><rt>do-cʽih-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>麦基洗德</rb><rt>Mah-kyi-si-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>迎接</rb><rt>nying-tsih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>祖宗个</rb><rt>tsu-tsong-go</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>祭司个</rb><rt>tsi-s-go</rt><rb>职份</rb><rt>tsih-veng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>全备</rb><rt>djün-be</rt></ruby>，（<ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>属下</rb><rt>joh-ʽô</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>，）<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt><rb>何用</rb><rt>ʽo-yüong</rt><rb>别个</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>麦基洗德</u>个</rb><rt>Mah-kyi-si-teh-go</rt><rb>班次</rb><rt>pæn-tsʽ</rt></ruby>」<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>出来</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亚伦</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-leng-go</rt><rb>班次</rb><rt>pæn-tsʽ</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>祭司个</rb><rt>Tsi-s-go</rt><rb>职份</rb><rt>tsih-veng</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>更改</rb><rt>keng-kæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>自然</rb><rt>z-jün</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>更改</rb><rt>keng-kæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>指点个</rb><rt>ts-tin-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>别个</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>侍值</rb><rt>z-dzih</rt><rb>祭坛</rb><rt>tsi-dæn</rt><rb>过</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>明明</rb><rt>ming-ming</rt><rb>出于</rb><rt>cʽih-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>并</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>讲着</rb><rt>kông-djôh</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>职份</rb><rt>tsih-veng</rt><rb>过</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>更加</rb><rt>keng-kô</rt><rb>显然</rb><rt>hyin-jün</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>别个</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>Tsi-s</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>麦基洗德</rb><rt>Mah-kyi-si-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>出来</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt><rb>号令个</rb><rt>ʽao-ling-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>呒穷尽</rb><rt>m-gyüong-dzing</rt><rb>活命个</rb><rt>weh-ming-go</rt><rb>能干</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>设立个</rb><rt>shih-lih-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>永远</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>Tsi-s</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>麦基洗德</u>个</rb><rt>Mah-kyi-si-teh-go</rt><rb>班次</rb><rt>pæn-tsʽ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>前头个</rb><rt>zin-deo-go</rt><rb>号令</rb><rt>ʽao-ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>懦弱</rb><rt>no-ziah</rt><rb>无用</rb><rt>vu-yüong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>废掉了</rb><rt>fi-diao-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> （<ruby><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>全备</rb><rt>djün-be</rt></ruby>；）<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>说出</rb><rt>shih-cʽih</rt><rb>更加</rb><rt>keng-kô</rt><rb>好个</rb><rt>hao-go</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>亲近</rb><rt>tsʽing-gying</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, *lih-fah* peh-ko s-teh keh keng-kô hao-go siang-vông læ; *kʽen* Ky. 3. 24: *ʽôh* *wô*, tsih-yiu sô shih-lih keh keng-kô hao-go siang-vông *we* *s-teh* *nying* *djün-be*. 或话，律法不过使得箇更加好个想望来；看 Ky. 3. 24。或话，只有所设立箇更加好个想望会使得人全备。（原注）</sup>

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>再话</rb><rt>Tsæ-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>设立</rb><rt>*shih-lih*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>罚咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt></ruby>；——<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>设立</rb><rt>shih-lih</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>罚咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt><rb>设立</rb><rt>*shih-lih*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>罚咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>CÜ</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>罚咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再𣍐</rb><rt>tsæ-ve</rt><rb>反悔</rb><rt>fæn-hwe</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>永远</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>Tsi-s</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>麦基洗德</u>个</rb><rt>Mah-kyi-si-teh-go</rt><rb>班次</rb><rt>pæn-tsʽ</rt></ruby>。」——<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>*yia*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>更</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>约个</rb><rt>iah-go</rt><rb>保人</rb><rt>Pao-nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>长</rb><rt>dziang</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>永远</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>传授弗来个</rb><rt>djün-ziu-feh-læ-go</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>职份</rb><rt>tsih-veng</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>终于</rb><rt>cong-ü</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>走拢</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>时常</rb><rt>z-djông</rt><rb>活东</rb><rt>weh-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>替</rb><rt>tʽi</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>代求</rb><rt>dæ-gyiu</rt></ruby>。

### H. VIII.

<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>NÆN-KÆN</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>大规模</rb><rt>do-kwe-mo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>介个</rb><rt>ka-go</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>Tsi-s-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>威严</rb><rt>w̆e-nyin</rt><rb>主个</rb><rt>*Cü*-go</rt><rb>座位</rb><rt>zo-we</rt><rb>顺手边</rb><rt>jing-siu-pin</rt></ruby>；<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>圣善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>*di-fông*</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>帐篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>办事人</rb><rt>Bæn-z-nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>帐篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>搭</rb><rt>tah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>搭个</rb><rt>*tah-go*</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地下</rb><rt>di-ʽô</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>礼物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <sup>6</sup> <sup>7</sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>圣善个</rb><rt>sing-jün-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>*di-fông*</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup>
